# AWS privilege escalation: complete work register

This is the single status file for the AWS privilege escalation work. Regenerate it after changing any underlying TSV with `python3 work_aws_privesc_review/build_verification_status.py` followed by `python3 work_aws_privesc_review/build_worklog.py`. A catalog review is not end-to-end validation.

## Current counts

- Active catalog: 220 reviewed; 220 have at least one located test file. End-to-end suite states: {'executed_with_failures_global@0a4c1ff9b386': 220}.
- Final pytest run used CSPM commit `0a4c1ff9b38674bc9a5117861e03a351677c7aef` and HackTricks Cloud revision `e7a926f26506cfa9e5bb1016ff4dc9dc5c69a02c`. Its result applies to those exact revisions; later changes need their own validation.
- The full run's JUnit suite attributes recorded 159181 tests: 4916 failures, 104 errors, and 55 skips. The XML contains 158678 testcase elements, of which 103 carry an error; suite counters include pytest reporting beyond emitted testcase nodes. 6 skips were outside the skip allowlist (four Linux tmpfs cases on macOS, one explicit SQLite transition case, and one unavailable local Trivy cache); their exact test names and reasons are in `final-suite-result.json`.
- Separate focused runs at later exact revisions: Current catalog and route binding focus passed 23/23 on CSPM `396fa9992148b401622e60415e139f71e43cc76e` with HackTricks Cloud `e91f99c9915f4b778e8044daa1033d4cbb64868b` (JUnit SHA-256 `afba63d82e5d21f0495351821b76ff09fe5f9d733ce9a54c96c579c969e5dd28`); ECS route, current review contract, and exact catalog history passed 13/13 on CSPM `66014e196d62262104b8deab5290bd5f33b9f756` with HackTricks Cloud `4393380a88845b42bd208d3adbb6d61ad89f02fb` (JUnit SHA-256 `719180ce527198fab32bfb4eda28d19b81373d2d95defdefc4881d96c9c3ecba`); Main catalog binding and history focus passed 51/51 on CSPM `5b5aa6a7da85105b28e3ab5bf2b163de439b39ff` with HackTricks Cloud `4393380a88845b42bd208d3adbb6d61ad89f02fb` (JUnit SHA-256 `6c34e9390e2ff04c67d6a73a407ccf01121440cf04e28d109352aab5cecbb9ac`). These runs cover only their named test selections, not the full suite.
- Separate performance gate at CSPM `66014e196d62262104b8deab5290bd5f33b9f756` with HackTricks Cloud `4393380a88845b42bd208d3adbb6d61ad89f02fb`: 3 passed, 2 failed, 0 errors, and 1 expected skip (JUnit SHA-256 `0cffbae4f96c1b8e46931056f85055c6d2a618e1545561096a4a311848bdcd75`). The 1x run used 412 MiB and a repeat used 407 MiB, above the 400 MiB limit; a pre-PR baseline at `bddbcbd90bec3784845ef106a413e8e502b4ae53` used 373 MiB with the same 1570 checks and 20367 findings. The AWS 10x test took 481.73 seconds against a 20-second limit. The timed run overlapped unrelated 10x benchmark processes; an isolated result is pending.
- After the IAM cache change, the performance gate at CSPM `0520e7f187d8d7c33c8f67424991054e4490974d` with HackTricks Cloud `4393380a88845b42bd208d3adbb6d61ad89f02fb` still failed 2/2 tests (JUnit SHA-256 `a39df06582a4a150b197a5ae5c85b0e1f9e5a563cc4171310e313b8e42b85356`). The 1x run used 412 MiB against a 400 MiB limit. The AWS 10x run took 33.04 seconds against a 20-second limit, with 3156 principals and 813 edges. The earlier timed run took 481.73 seconds. Three other CPU-heavy benchmarks ran at the same time, so this is not an isolated timing result.
- Paired direct 1x memory repeats used the same command at baseline `bddbcbd90bec3784845ef106a413e8e502b4ae53` and current `0520e7f187d8d7c33c8f67424991054e4490974d`. Baseline RSS values were 401, 391, 395 MiB; current values were 415, 404, 398 MiB against a 400 MiB limit. Their ranges overlap, so the earlier single 373 MiB baseline result does not establish a source-specific memory increase. The memory gate remains failed on measured current runs. Six direct benchmark command outputs in the agent tool transcript; no JUnit or log file was saved.
- A later isolated performance gate passed 2/2 tests on CSPM `70a311b68143ddff2935162de82656dda9ab85df` with HackTricks Cloud `4393380a88845b42bd208d3adbb6d61ad89f02fb` (JUnit SHA-256 `128be882ba761631688bd1a2d285efcdb03ecbe42872b09e034e802958b6feea`). The 1x run used 398 MiB against a 400 MiB limit; the AWS 10x run took 9.67 seconds against a 20-second limit, with 3156 principals and 813 edges. Earlier failed runs remain recorded at their exact revisions. This focused gate does not replace the frozen full-suite result.
- ID-specific graph test audit: 84 have a recorded passing exact case, 136 have a located but partial case, and 0 have no ID-specific case located. The exact test evidence and remaining gaps for every ID are in `route-case-coverage.tsv`; these coverage states do not establish whether a route can be used in an account.
- Among the 136 partial routes, 136 name a positive assertion, 136 name a negative assertion, and 38 name an unknown-result assertion. These are located assertions, not recorded passing exact graph cases.
- Partial routes without a named positive assertion: none.
- Partial routes without a named negative assertion: none.
- External evidence: 58 active techniques reference `aws_privesc_challenge_evidence`; 55 require a trusted live-effect witness for a conditional edge and 3 Glue routes also have a native conditional path. The IAM and resource prerequisites still run automatically; missing live-effect proof remains UNKNOWN.
- Retired cohort: 0 candidates unresolved, 333 exclusions verified, 29 restored or implemented.
- Exclusion source lineage: 22 of 22 documented path or anchor discrepancies have an exact historical-source binding; 0 still require the follow-up listed below. These are documentation bindings, not evidence that a route should be active.
- Exclusion lineage semantic review: 22 of 22 path-drift entries have a recorded current-page disposition; 0 remain to review. 4 exclusion reasons were corrected in HackTricks Cloud and the matching CSPM catalog. The current exclusions remain in force; review status does not establish that no other false negatives exist.
- Additional routes: 0 candidates unresolved, 19 exclusions verified, 0 implemented.
- HackTricks pages: 137 reviewed, 0 still need sign-off.
- Source and data presentation pins: 0 files differ from the pinned source hashes in the current source manifest.
